Result: 在 21 个文本与多模态模型上评测：在 5 个白盒源模型上取得 76%-100% 的越狱发现率，比基线高出最多 48 个百分点；优化后的模板可零样本迁移至开源权重及 6 个专有目标模型，平均攻击成功率（ASR）与 top-5 集成攻击成功率（EASR）分别达到 69.6%/92.6% 和 44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: 通过将内部安全神经元激活转化为连续、可微分且可在预填充阶段获取的反馈，NeuronFuzz 有效解决了响应级反馈昂贵且稀疏的问题，在提升越狱发现效率与跨模型迁移能力上均显著优于现有基线，为 LLM 安全评估提供了更高效的白盒模糊测试方案。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [327] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，用于二值化神经网络（BNN）的设计与优化，并在此基础上提出一种跨抽象层级的全局权重剪枝方法。实验表明，在VGG11上该方法可在保持精度的同时达到70%的剪枝率，显著优于二值化设置下仅41%的现有最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的极端压缩（直至完全二值化）能大幅降低内存占用和算术复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等边缘硬件上部署。尽管将二值化与剪枝结合有望带来额外的效率提升，但现有剪枝策略并不适用于二值化表示，且很少能转化为实际的硬件节省，因此需要更适合二值化网络的剪枝机制与研究框架。

Method: 提出一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，内置冻结（freezing）和剪枝机制，用于设计和优化二值化神经网络，支持对现有先进方法的快速、可复现评估以及新方法的快速原型开发。在此基础上，提出一种新的剪枝方法，该方法在跨抽象层级上衡量学习参数的相对重要性，通过全局加权机制在模型精度与剪枝率之间取得更优的折中。

Result: 所提出的全局加权剪枝机制在模型精度与剪枝率之间始终获得更优的权衡：在VGG11上达到70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而二值化设置下现有最优方法仅能达到41%的剪枝率。

Conclusion: 全局加权剪枝方法显著优于现有二值化剪枝策略，在保持精度的同时实现了更高的剪枝率；所提框架也促进了研究表明的可复现性与新方法的快速迭代，为二值化神经网络的高效压缩提供了实用工具与算法支撑。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [328] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明有限次Newton-Schulz迭代在非光滑非凸优化中对Muon优化器并非近似误差而是有益成分：它将不连续的极分解映射平滑为奇异值上的Lipschitz映射，使Muon可被视为具有平滑谱势的在线学习器，只需对数深度的迭代即可保证收敛到驻点，而使用精确极分解的Muon反而可能不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon通过少数几步Newton-Schulz迭代近似正交化动量，成为大语言模型预训练中矩阵参数优化的强力工具。但现有理论要么用精确极因子替换该迭代，要么将有限深度视为近似误差，导致理论保证中Muon实际运行的迭代只能损害收敛性。这产生了理论与实际之间的脱节：实践中Newton-Schulz的有限深度可能正是优势来源，但缺乏理论解释。

Method: 作者通过online-to-nonconvex转换框架分析Muon，将更新规则视为在线学习器，并把其遗憾界转化为驻点性保证。关键洞察是：有限次Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极分解映射平滑为奇异值空间上的Lipschitz映射，从而使Muon成为携带平滑谱势的在线学习器；该平滑性恰是online-to-nonconvex转换所需的性质。作者据此证明Newton-Schulz深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证非光滑非凸优化收敛到驻点，并将论证推广到具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Result: 证明了有限Newton-Schulz迭代在非光滑非凸优化中是有益的：其深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证收敛到驻点；而采用精确极分解更新的Muon可能无法收敛。所得样本复杂度界与非光滑非凸优化的最佳已知结果匹配，并且在光滑非凸优化中除问题相关因子外是最优的。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，有限次Newton-Schulz迭代的"近似误差"实际上是不可或缺的平滑机制，它使Muon在非光滑非凸场景下获得收敛保证；该结果弥合了理论与实践之间的鸿沟，并揭示了更一般的原理——任何具有类似平滑性质的谱映射都能提供同样的收敛保障。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [329] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于代数多重网格加速的高效标签传播框架（AMELS），通过快速构建邻接图和使用代数多重网格求解器替代传统随机游走迭代，在低标注成本下显著降低大规模高维数据集上的计算与内存开销，并提升对超参数的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型依赖大量带标签数据，但大规模数据集的人工标注昂贵且耗时。标签传播作为半监督学习技术虽可利用少量标注样本，但在大规模、高维数据集上受限于计算成本和内存约束，可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出 AMELS 框架，包含两部分改进：一是快速构建邻接图以降低图构建开销；二是引入代数多重网格求解器作为迭代求解器，替代标签传播中常用的随机游走迭代。凭借多重网格的多层特性，AMELS 可在单个多重网格循环内将标签信息传播至任意规模的图。

Result: 与现有实现相比，AMELS 在运行时上实现显著缩减，并且在运行时间和分类精度两方面对超参数选择更为鲁棒。该方法可在大规模图像数据集上实现高效标签传播，即便仅使用少量标注样本也能产生准确标签。

Conclusion: AMELS 有效克服了标签传播在大规模高维数据上的可扩展性瓶颈，在显著加速的同时保持鲁棒性和准确性，为少样本条件下的大规模图像标注提供了一种高效可行方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [330] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Best-of-N采样选取响应之前向奖励分数注入校准噪声这一单一干预，同时解决奖励黑客攻击与人类偏好数据隐私保护两大问题。作者提出两种方法：PrivBoN（利用合适尺度的Gumbel噪声同时实现ε-差分隐私与KL正则化对齐）和PrivITP（结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP），并证明二者均可达到信息论skyline下界；实验表明两种方法具备缩放单调性，且PrivITP在同等隐私水平下匹配或优于PrivBoN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N采样是最简单且被广泛部署的推理时对齐策略，但存在两个相互独立的问题：(1) 奖励黑客攻击——被选中的响应可能利用代理奖励模型的错误；(2) 用于训练奖励模型的敏感人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。作者的核心动机是寻找单一的干预手段，同时缓解这两个问题，使私有化对齐不牺牲对齐性能。

Method: 核心方法是在选择响应之前向奖励分数添加校准噪声。其一是PrivBoN：向奖励分数注入合适尺度的Gumbel噪声，该噪声同时提供ε-差分隐私并实现KL正则化对齐；关键结论是当隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私所要求的噪声正是后悔最优（regret-optimal）的正则化，隐私由此不带来额外的对齐代价。其二是PrivITP：鉴于ε*依赖未知的覆盖率系数，它结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，其隐私成本与候选响应数n无关，并清晰地将正则化参数与隐私参数解耦，在skyline之上仅引入噪声放大项。

Result: 实验覆盖多种语言模型、数据集和奖励模型，验证了理论结论：PrivBoN与PrivITP具有缩放单调性（而BoN在超过临界响应数n后性能退化）；在等价隐私水平下PrivITP匹配或优于PrivBoN，且在强隐私保护区间获益最大。

Conclusion: 单一干预——在选取前向奖励分数添加校准噪声——即可同时化解奖励黑客与隐私泄露两大问题。当隐私预算足够大时，为满足隐私所需的噪声即是理想的对齐正则化，隐私保护不产生额外对齐代价；而在实际中更具鲁棒性的PrivITP通过解耦参数并使隐私成本与n无关，展现出更好的实用性与扩展性。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [331] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息下限"（information floor）度量，将块草稿器拒绝率分解为"缺失块内路径信息"与"对可观测信息建模不完善"两部分，并通过多目标模型实验证明短程条件化对每槽接受率的主导价值，提案质量差距则体现在下限之上的"模型差距"中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块草稿器（block drafter）在单次前向传播中提出多个 token，但其拒绝率来自两种混杂损失：缺失块内路径信息和难以对可观测信息建模。仅凭接受长度无法区分二者，因而需要一个新的度量框架，将"信息不足"与"模型自身缺陷"分离，以正确评估草稿器质量。

Method: 定义"信息下限"为在指定条件化阶数下无法避免的最小期望拒绝率；拒绝率高于该下限的部分称为"模型差距"（model gap）。二者均从目标模型的自回归回滚（target rollouts）中估计，覆盖四个领域、四个开源目标模型（含 Qwen3-4B）及一个前沿 API 目标模型，并用独立的互信息分析交叉验证短程条件化的重要性。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 全并行信息下限在 Qwen3-4B 的最后槽位上达到 0.286，即便最佳提案每槽接受率上限也仅为 71%；(2) 仅一个已实现 token 的条件化即可消除该下限的 86%–100%，表明信息高度局部化，且互信息分析独立证实了这一点；(3) 现有草稿器远高于其下限——最后槽位的模型差距占 DFlash 拒绝的 43%–64%，占 DSpark oracle 条件化拒绝的 85%–92%。

Conclusion: 这些发现将短程条件化的价值与提案质量分离开来：块内当前其提升空间主要来自缩小模型差距，而非继续堆叠条件化信息；该分解框架为未来草稿器设计与评估提供了归一化的基准。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [332] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpEmbed 框架，利用结构化且隐私保护的工单元数据（不含工单文本）为 LLM 云服务学习紧凑的"运维指纹"嵌入，并通过时序对比学习、跨视图重建及代际序数正则化捕获模型-时间窗口的八通道运维信号。在 Google Cloud 超 33,000 条生产工单、7 个 LLM 家族、26 个月数据上验证，该方法恢复了可解释的家族/版本结构、提升了留一模型预测的运维预报能力、在早期窗口数据有限时依然有效，并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管式 LLM 服务已进入真实生产系统，但模型选型和服务规划仍依赖能力基准测试，而这些基准几乎无法揭示部署后的实际运维行为。因此需要一种能刻画 LLM 云服务部署后运维表现的方法。

Method: 提出 OpEmbed 框架：将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道运维签名，不依赖工单文本，仅使用结构化、隐私保护的工单元数据；接着通过时序对比学习、跨视图重建和代际序数正则化学习低维运维表征。

Result: 在 Google Cloud 超过 33,000 条生产工单、7 个 LLM 家族、26 个月的数据上，OpEmbed 恢复了可解释的家族级和版本级结构；相比非学习基线，改善了留一模型（leave-one-model-out）的运维预测；在早期窗口数据有限时仍保持有效性；并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed 为模型上线、支持就绪评估和运维监控提供了实用工具，作者总结了从构建与评估该工具过程中获得的实践经验，表明运维嵌入可作为能力基准之外的有效补充，用于生产环境中的 LLM 服务运营。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [333] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CG4AI框架，通过将多个AI模型进行凸组合并在组合输出上施加线性约束，实现可满足预定义规则/约束的预测器。主线性规划求解最优混合权重，定价子问题依据LP对偶变量生成新模型，切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。在MNIST（约束学习、对抗鲁棒性、纠正误分类、输出重标记四种用途）和多商品流问题（链路容量约束）上验证，生成的可行预测器准确率优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练仅最小化数据集上的损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义的规则或约束；而在自动驾驶、网络路由等真实应用中，此类可行性保证至关重要。

Method: 提出CG4AI框架：构造AI模型的凸组合并对组合输出施加线性约束。主线性规划（LP）确定最优混合权重；定价子问题利用LP对偶变量指导新模型的生成，聚焦于违反最严重的约束；切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。

Result: 在MNIST上展示约束的四种用途（仅从约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、纠正误分类示例、强制输出重标记），在多商品流问题上强制神经网络路由预测器满足链路容量约束。在MNIST和标准SNDLIB基准网络上的实验表明，CG4AI能可靠地生成可行预测器，且准确率优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI提供了一种将线性约束嵌入机器学习预测的有效框架，在保证输出可行性的同时提升准确性，可推广到多种约束型预测任务。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [334] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"潜在诊断分类法"（Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy）的框架，通过维度优化分类器、提取约29%训练样本构成的潜在支持向量、并分析可改变预测标签的标记及其攻击幅度，构建一个端到端的诊断分类体系。该体系将提示分为"可安全依赖""启发式偏差""启发式覆盖""上下文不足"等区域，并针对每类给出修复策略。在公开的提示注入数据集上，该框架发现约77%的高置信决策在移除单个标记后不再稳健，且这种脆弱性可区分为置信度校准失败与真正可被利用的捷径两类故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器（尤其是语言模型上的安全防护分类器）经常给出过度自信的决策，但缺乏系统性的方法来识别哪些高置信决策真正可信任、哪些存在脆弱性。作者希望构建一个可解释的诊断框架，将分类器的"决策置信度"与"实际稳健性"联系起来，从而为安全防护场景提供端到端的分诊指南。

Method: 框架包含三个步骤：(i) 构建维度优化的分类器——嵌入维度通过交叉验证性能经验性选择，而非预先固定；(ii) 定位一个较小的潜在支持向量集合（约占训练样本的29%），代表可识别"会改变分类器预测标签的标记"的高影响力提示；(iii) 利用这些标记及其关联的攻击幅度构建诊断分类法。该分类法将提示划分为需要不同处理的区域：可安全依赖（Rely Safely）、启发式偏差（Heuristic Bias）、启发式覆盖（Heuristic Override）以及需要人工/安全审查的上下文不足（Insufficient Context）。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，约77%的高置信分类决策无法通过移除单个标记保持稳健；该脆弱性分离为两种截然不同的失败模式：一种是置信度校准失败（confidence calibration failure），另一种是真正可被利用的捷径（genuinely exploitable shortcut）。框架还为分类法的每个区域提供了修复建议。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可操作的、端到端的诊断流程，能够将不可靠的高置信决策系统性地识别出来并划分到不同问题类别，进而针对每类问题给出相应的修复或人工审查策略。这为语言模型安全防护层的可解释性和鲁棒性评估提供了实用方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [335] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedCMAPSS，一个基于 NASA C-MAPSS 数据集、面向联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计的标准评测基准，定义了五个从 IID 到极端统计异质性的标准化任务，并系统评估了多种联邦优化算法与神经网络架构，公开了源码和数据划分，为联邦预测性维护研究提供可复现的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的预测性健康管理是工业 4.0 的关键技术，但 RUL 估计模型的开发常受限于运行至失效数据的稀缺；联邦学习虽可在不共享传感器数据的情况下协作训练模型，但此前研究缺乏统一的评测框架，导致结果难以比较与复现。

Method: 基于常用的 NASA C-MAPSS 数据集构建 FedCMAPSS 基准，设计五个标准化任务以模拟真实工业挑战（从理想 IID 设置到极端统计异质性），并针对多种神经网络架构系统评估了最先进的联邦优化算法，同时公开源码与数据划分以保证可复现性。

Result: 建立了可复现的基准基线和标准化的任务划分，系统对比了不同联邦优化算法在多种架构下的表现，并公开源码与数据划分，为后续联邦预测性维护方案的开发与比较提供了标准基础。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS填补了联邦 RUL 估计领域缺乏统一评测框架的空白，通过标准化任务、系统评估与公开资源，为工业场景下联邦预测性维护的研究提供了可参照的标准平台，有助于推动该方向的可比较与可复现发展。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [336] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeoTriFuse，一种可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，将缺失率建模为显式的可靠性信号以动态调节模态贡献，用于新生儿监护数据中的死亡风险预测，在极端类别不平衡与多尺度时间动态条件下取得竞争性性能（F1 0.6736，AUROC 0.9454）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡风险预测面临极端类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质、多尺度时间动态以及大量数据缺失等挑战。现有多模态方法通常将缺失视为预处理问题，忽略了缺失本身蕴含的可靠性信息，因此需要一种将缺失显式建模为可靠性信号、动态调节模态融合的框架。

Method: NeoTriFuse整合静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器和患者级统计摘要三类模态，通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节各模态贡献；同时联合优化死亡预测主任务与辅助住院时长目标，并对缺失异质性进行显式建模而非简单预处理。

Result: NeoTriFuse取得F1为0.6736±0.0216、AUROC为0.9454±0.0056的竞争性性能。消融实验表明局部-全局时间架构与患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在观测完整度异质时进一步改善阈值依赖指标；敏感性分析显示对邻近超参数设置保持稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的多模态融合是面向真实临床缺失条件下新生儿死亡预测的实用方案，验证了将缺失作为可靠性信号纳入融合机制的有效性与稳定性。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [337] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SF-GNN 是一个轻量级、架构无关的图神经网络公平性框架，通过在消息传递前对高偏差风险的边进行随机过滤，从源头抑制结构偏差传播，并在五个基准上获得更好的公平性-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使在节点特征中去除敏感属性，GNN 仍可能因图拓扑与消息传递在敏感同质性（sensitive homophily）下传播组相关信号而产生不公平行为。现有公平感知 GNN 方法主要在全球层面对表示或预测分布施加约束，未显式控制聚合过程中偏见信息传播的局部结构通路。

Method: SF-GNN 结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器（枢纽参与 hub participation、三角闭合 triadic closure）识别易偏边，并在每一步消息传递中引入随机边过滤（stochastic edge filtering），选择性地降低权重或移除这些边，同时保留其余图结构。训练中进一步加入带预热调度的统计平价正则项（statistical-parity regularizer）以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，SF-GNN 在保持有竞争力的预测性能的同时实现了一致的公平性提升，相比近期公平感知 GNN 基线取得了更好的公平性-精度权衡。

Conclusion: SF-GNN 提供了一种架构无关、轻量的方案，从图的结构源头（局部传播路径）而非全局分布层面缓解不公平，为公平 GNN 提供了一种更直接有效的抑制结构偏差的途径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [338] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FairGIN，一种面向扩张中共享单车系统的公平感知图神经网络，通过模拟网络扩张、知识迁移和公平性优化，在保持预测精度的同时减少基于收入的分配差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统持续扩张带来两大挑战：一是新建站点缺乏历史骑行记录，导致图模型在演化网络上训练与推理分布不匹配（冷启动问题）；二是历史需求可能编码结构性不平等，低收入社区的低骑行量往往反映基础设施接入受限而非潜在需求疲软，直接在这些数据上训练会加剧现有出行不公平。

Method: FairGIN 包含三个组件：(1) 扩张模拟增量训练（Expansion-Simulated Increment Training），在训练中随机模拟网络扩张以缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) 基于注意力的知识迁移（Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer），结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将表示从数据丰富的现有站点迁移到数据稀疏的新站点；(3) 公平感知优化（Fairness-Aware Optimization），引入按收入分层正则化与公平校准的部署评分，支持更包容的站点布设。

Result: 在纽约和西雅图数据集上的实验表明，FairGIN 在多种扩张场景下取得最先进的预测精度，同时在不牺牲整体系统效率的前提下显著减少基于收入的差距。

Conclusion: FairGIN 证明可以通过联合建模冷启动预测和公平性约束，使扩张中的共享单车系统既保持高效需求预测，又能缓解结构性的出行不平等，为公平导向的城市基础设施规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [339] [Distributed Training using an Intelligent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26453)
*Nihar Shah,Ben Blier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出让广域网（WAN）成为分布式训练中主动参与者的方案：在系统侧利用组播复制出站流量、在线FPGA聚合入站流量以缓解出入口瓶颈，在算法侧开发优化框架生成围绕网络拓扑的丰富同步调度（旋转计算岛派系），以最大化信息交换，从而在低带宽、高延迟的WAN环境下缩小与同地训练之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨广域网进行分布式训练极具挑战：计算岛之间连续的参数交换受限于有限带宽、高延迟和不均匀的网络拓扑。虽然数据中心内部已利用组播和在线FPGA技术提升训练效率，但这些技术尚未扩展到WAN场景，因此需要探索如何让网络主动参与训练以突破该瓶颈。

Method: 从系统与算法两个层面展开。系统侧：复用并扩展数据中心内的两项技术——利用组播技术复制出站流量、利用在线FPGA聚合入站流量，以缓解出口与入口的带宽压力。算法侧：开发一个优化框架，围绕底层网络拓扑及上述技术生成丰富的同步调度方案（即在不同计算岛之间进行旋转式派系同步），以最大化信息交换效率。

Result: 在基于DoubleZero可编程WAN的九城市拓扑上进行验证，结果表明最优同步调度会随网络能力的差异而变化，且上述系统与算法技术的结合能够有效缩小分布式训练与同地训练（colocated training）这一黄金标准之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 使网络成为训练过程中的主动参与者——借助组播、在线FPGA等网络能力并配合面向拓扑的优化调度框架——能够显著改善WAN环境下的分布式训练性能，拉近与同地训练之间的差距，为跨地域大模型训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Distributed training across a wide area network (WAN) is challenging, as continuous parameter exchange by islands of compute is constrained by limited bandwidth, high latency, and uneven topology. We propose making the network an active participant in training. On the systems side, such networks should leverage (i) multicast technology to replicate outbound traffic and (ii) in-line FPGAs to aggregate inbound traffic, to ease egress and ingress bottlenecks. These technologies are used for training across workers within a data center, but this paper extends them to the WAN. On the algorithms side, we develop an optimization framework that produces rich synchronization schedules (namely, rotating cliques of islands) around the underlying network topology and these technologies, to maximize information exchange. Finally, we illustrate this on a nine-city topology modeled on the DoubleZero network, a live programmable WAN equipped with both technologies, and show how the optimal schedules shift with the network's capabilities. Together, these can narrow the gap to the gold standard of colocated training.

</details>


### [340] [Diff Mining: Logit Differences Reveal Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26462)
*Greg Kocher,Robert West,Clément Dumas,Julian Minder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diff Mining，一个简单有效的框架，通过比较微调模型与其基础模型的输出 logits 来识别微调模型学到了什么。该框架仅需输出 logits、无需模型内部结构，可扩展至大模型；通过两阶段流程（提取逐上下文 logit 差异 + 聚合信号）生成可解释的 token 集合作微调“指纹”。在微调域检测、注入偏差识别等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为改进和引入语言模型行为的黄金标准，但微调过程中究竟涌现了哪些行为往往不明确。随着模型能力增强，理解微调过程愈发重要，尤其是微调期间可能出现不期望的行为（如偏差），因此需要一种能够识别微调所学内容的审计工具。

Method: Diff Mining 框架由两个模块化阶段组成：(i) 在参考语料上提取微调模型与基础模型之间逐上下文的 logit 差异；(ii) 聚合这些差异信号以构造代表微调的可解释 token 集合。聚合阶段探索了两种方法：简单的 Top-K 频率方法和基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）的方法，后者可将多个微调目标分离成不同的 token 簇。该方法只需访问输出 logits，无需模型内部信息。

Result: 实验表明：在微调域检测任务上，Diff Mining 在识别相关 token 和下游性能方面显著优于最先进的模型差异方法（当可解释性代理可访问提取的 token 集合时）；在注入偏差的模型上，无需针对性探测即可识别超过三分之一的偏差。框架在多种设置下均有效。

Conclusion: Diff Mining 展示了一种仅凭输出 logits 即可检测微调目标的轻量级审计方法，有望发展成为检测微调目标（包括潜在不良行为）的审计工具，为模型安全与可解释性领域提供支撑。

Abstract: Finetuning has become the gold standard for refining existing behaviors and inducing new ones in language models, yet it often remains unclear exactly which behaviors emerge during this process. As models grow ever more capable, understanding finetuning better becomes increasingly important, particularly since unwanted behaviors may arise during finetuning. In this paper, we introduce Diff Mining, a simple yet effective framework for identifying what a finetuned model has learned by comparing its logits to those of its base model. Diff Mining effectively surfaces salient tokens that are amplified in the finetuned model, serving as a fingerprint of its training -- even on text unrelated to the finetuning domain. Unlike many existing model diffing methods which require model internals, Diff Mining only needs access to output logits and scales to large models. The framework consists of two modular stages: (i) extracting per-context logit differences between the finetuned and base models on a reference corpus, and (ii) aggregating the resulting signals to construct an interpretable token set representing the finetune. For aggregation, we explore both a simple Top-K frequency method and a Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF)-based approach for disentangling multiple finetuning objectives into distinct token clusters. Empirically, Diff Mining succeeds across diverse settings: on finetune domain detection, it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art model diffing methods both in identifying relevant tokens and in downstream performance when an interpretability agent is given access to the extracted token set; on models with injected biases, it identifies more than one third of the biases without targeted probing. Overall, our framework shows promise in developing auditing tools to detect finetuning objectives.

</details>


### [341] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH 是一个在强化学习（RL）中显式利用课程图（DAG）进行主动学习的课程学习框架：先通过采样多样化课程路径扩大覆盖范围，再将训练资源重新分配至尚未掌握的区域，从而提升鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多 RL 环境之间存在前置/先修关系（如难度递增的编辑或参数增量），由此可构成一个有向无环课程图（DAG）。然而现有方法通常仅隐式利用该结构，作者认为显式建模课程图可以改善训练效果。

Method: 提出 PATH 框架，在课程图上进行主动学习。具体包含两个阶段：1) 通过采样多样的课程路径来扩大对图的覆盖；2) 将训练重心重新分配至仍未掌握（unmastered）的区域，实现有针对性的训练资源调度。

Result: 在多个不同环境中验证，PATH 显式利用课程图结构，取得了较强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模并主动利用环境间的课程图结构，能够有效地指导 RL 训练过程，在多样化环境中实现稳健且可泛化的学习性能。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [342] [Shared Actors Need Not Share Critics: Effects of Value Mismatch in Parallel Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26481)
*Zhenya Liu,Yang Meng,Zhuokai Zhao,Xuefeng Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究并行强化学习中多个环境共用一个critic所导致的“价值不匹配”（value mismatch）问题：不同环境对同一状态可能赋予不同的期望回报，共享critic被迫调和这些不同的价值目标，从而系统地扭曲各环境内的优势估计和策略更新。作者用多环境bandit模型刻画了这一机理，并提出最小干预方案——仅向critic输入环境索引（多头条件critic），使其能够分离价值目标。实验表明该方法在CartPole、MuJoCo、BipedalWalker及全部16个Procgen游戏中显著提升学习稳定性与回报，其中Procgen未见过的600关聚合归一化回报提升40.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行RL中（如程序化生成关卡、随机动力学、课程学习），同一策略在多个环境上并行训练，实现中通常所有采样环境共用一个critic。然而不同环境可能对critic可见的同一输入赋予不同的期望回报；缺乏环境信息的critic必须调和这些不同的价值目标，使各个环境内的采样优势被系统性偏移，进而扭曲策略更新。现有度量（如标量估计器方差）无法解释这一退化机制，因此需要从理论上揭示并解决该问题。

Method: 作者先以包含多个环境且共享同一最优动作的bandit模型为例，数学刻画价值不匹配如何重新分配采样到的策略更新：强化无益动作、削弱甚至逆转有益动作。理论证明，不使用基线、使用共享价值、使用各环境专属价值这三种oracle过程在固定策略下具有相同的平均logit更新且收敛到相同最优策略，但实际学习路径可能有显著差异。基于此，作者提出最小干预：仅向critic提供记录的（logged）环境索引，使其能够分离价值目标（multihead conditional critic），并在CartPole、MuJoCo、BipedalWalker和Procgen上开展受控实验验证。

Result: CartPole与MuJoCo受控实验暴露了理论预测的偏移价值、偏移优势及性能差距；在更复杂的BipedalWalker和Procgen中，同一干预带来更稳定的学习和更高回报；在全部16个Procgen游戏中，多头条件critic使每个游戏600个未见关卡上的聚合归一化回报提升40.8%。

Conclusion: 理论确认价值不匹配是critic共享导致随机学习动态退化的直接机制，其影响无法仅由标量估计器方差捕获；实验表明仅对critic进行环境索引条件化在并行强化学习中具有广泛有效性。

Abstract: When a single policy is trained in parallel across multiple environments of the same task, such as procedurally generated levels, randomized dynamics, or curricula, implementations commonly use one critic across all sampled environments. Yet different environments can assign different expected returns to the same input visible to the critic. A critic without environment information must then reconcile distinct value targets, systematically shifting the sampled advantages within individual environments. Using illustrative bandit models with multiple environments and a common optimal arm, we characterize how this value mismatch redistributes sampled policy updates, reinforcing unhelpful actions while attenuating or even reversing useful ones. The oracle processes using no baseline, the shared value, or the value specific to the sampled environment have the same mean logit update at a fixed policy and converge to the same optimal policy, yet their realized learning paths can differ sharply. The analysis motivates a minimal intervention: give only a logged environment index to the critic so that it can separate the value targets. Controlled CartPole and MuJoCo experiments expose the predicted shifted values, advantages, and performance gaps. In the more complex BipedalWalker and Procgen settings, the same intervention yields more stable learning and higher returns. Across all $16$ Procgen games, the multihead conditional critic improves aggregate normalized return on $600$ unseen levels per game by $40.8\%$. In conclusion, the theory identifies value mismatch as a direct mechanism through which critic sharing can degrade stochastic learning dynamics, not captured by scalar estimator variance alone, and the experiments show that conditioning on an index is broadly effective in parallel reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [343] [Bayesian methods and Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms for curve reconstruction and point cloud data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26490)
*Asir Intesar Tushar,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种全贝叶斯框架，用于表示点云数据并重建闭合曲线，同时提供不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云数据虽能提供详细的几何描述，但受数据量大、定位噪声和缺失信息的影响而难以分析；此外，现有重建流程通常只返回单一的最优拟合结构，无法量化不确定性。

Method: 将观测点建模为潜在位置的带噪扰动，且这些潜在位置被约束位于底层曲线上，并通过非参数先验对曲线进行正则化；后验推断采用一系列针对点云特征定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器完成。

Result: 在合成示例和真实LiDAR数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法能够实现准确的重建，并对恢复出的曲线提供量化不确定性。

Conclusion: 该全贝叶斯框架有效解决了点云分析中的噪声、数据量大和不确定性问题，相比传统单一最优拟合方法，能同时给出精确的重建结果和可靠的不确定性度量。

Abstract: Point-cloud data routinely captured by modern imaging and sensor technologies provide detailed geometric descriptions of objects and environments, but their analysis is hindered by large data volumes, localization noise, and missing information. In addition, existing point-cloud reconstruction pipelines typically return a single best-fit structure without uncertainty quantification. We introduce a fully Bayesian framework for representing point-cloud data and reconstructing closed curves, in which observed points are modeled as noisy perturbations of latent locations constrained to lie on the underlying curve that is regularized by a non-parametric prior. Posterior inference in our framework is carried out using a series of Markov chain Monte Carlo samplers tailored to point-cloud characteristics. Numerical experiments, including synthetic examples and real-world LiDAR datasets, show accurate reconstructions and quantified uncertainty over the recovered curves.

</details>


### [344] [A Unified Framework for Fair and Personalized Decentralized Learning under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26493)
*Krishnendu S. Tharakan,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架DMFL-SQ，在去中心化学习中同时实现图结构个性化、不可知公平性和通信效率（稀疏化+量化+事件触发），并在一般非凸目标下以O(T^{-1/2})的莫罗包络驻点率收敛，同时给出PAC-Bayes泛化界；实验表明该算法在显著降低通信的同时保持性能并提升客户端公平性，说明个性化、公平性与通信效率可共同达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习无需中心协调器，虽提升可扩展性、隐私与鲁棒性，但加剧了三大根本挑战：客户端统计异质性、客户端级性能公平性以及严格的通信约束。作者由此提出核心问题：在有限通信下，去中心化学习能达到多公平？现有工作缺乏将公平性与通信效率统一考量的框架。

Method: 构建一个统一框架，融合基于图的个性化、不可知（agnostic）公平性与压缩事件触发通信。提出新算法DMFL-SQ：一种去中心化多任务学习算法，在通信图上耦合个性化模型训练与不可知混合公平目标，并通过稀疏化、量化与事件触发同步三管齐下降低通信开销；分析上结合非凸优化收敛分析（期望平方Moreau包络驻点）与PAC-Bayes泛化理论。

Result: 针对一般非凸目标证明DMFL-SQ在稀疏、量化及事件触发通信下仍达到O(T^{-1/2})的期望平方Moreau包络驻点收敛率；推导公平感知混合目标的PAC-Bayes泛化保证；在CIFAR-10与真实异构MUSMET EEG数据集上的实验表明，该方法大幅降低通信量，同时保持预测性能并提升客户端间公平性。

Conclusion: 理论与实验共同证明：在去中心化学习中，个性化、公平性与通信效率可以同时实现，且不牺牲主导收敛速率。

Abstract: Decentralized learning systems aim to collaboratively train models across multiple clients without relying on a central coordinator. While decentralization improves scalability, privacy, and robustness, it also exacerbates three fundamental challenges: statistical heterogeneity across clients, fairness in client-level performance, and stringent communication constraints. This raises a natural question: \emph{how fair can decentralized learning be under limited communication?} We address this question by presenting a unified framework for decentralized learning under communication constraints, bringing together graph-based personalization, agnostic fairness, and compressed event-triggered communication. Specifically, we propose a new algorithm DMFL-SQ, a decentralized multi-task learning algorithm that couples personalized model training over a communication graph with an agnostic mixture fairness objective, while reducing communication through sparsification, quantization, and event-triggered synchronization. We establish convergence guarantees for general non-convex objectives and show that DMFL-SQ achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate in expected squared Moreau-envelope stationarity despite sparse, quantized, and event-triggered communication. We further derive PAC-Bayes generalization guarantees for the fairness-aware mixture objective. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and the real heterogeneous MUSMET EEG dataset demonstrate that DMFL-SQ substantially reduces communication while maintaining predictive performance and improving fairness across clients. Together, our theoretical and empirical results show that personalization, fairness, and communication efficiency can be jointly achieved in decentralized learning while preserving the dominant convergence rate.

</details>


### [345] [A Single Suffix to Break Them All: Basin-Aware Jailbreaks for Merged Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26506)
*Yu Zhe,Yixin Tan,Junhao Wei,Wang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示模型合并中一个被忽视的越狱风险：即使所有构成模型都经过独立的安全对齐，合并后的模型仍可能因预训练基座模型的存在而暴露出可被利用的越狱漏洞。作者提出Basin-Aware Jailbreak（BAJ）方法，将越狱后缀生成建模为合并空间上的极小极大优化，从而构造出可跨共享同一预训练骨干的合并模型家族迁移的对抗后缀，并在多种骨干与合并设置下取得高迁移成功率且能抵抗现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要将模型合并的安全风险归因于不安全的构成模型，隐含假设合并多个已对齐模型能保持安全性；然而作者发现模型合并会暴露源于预训练基座模型的越狱风险，即使所有构成模型均单独对齐。这一被忽视的现象催生了新的威胁设定，需要系统研究。

Method: 提出Basin-Aware Jailbreak（BAJ）：将越狱后缀生成形式化为合并空间上的极小极大（min-max）优化问题，在未知确切合并系数和构成检查点的条件下，生成可跨共享同一预训练骨干的合并模型家族迁移的对抗性后缀。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和合并设置下的实验表明，BAJ持续取得较高的越狱迁移成功率，并且在现有防御手段下依然有效。

Conclusion: 模型合并本身即可引入源自预训练基座的越狱风险，不能仅依赖构成模型的对齐状态评估合并安全性；BAJ展示的攻击范式说明合并模型共享的基座是一个值得防护的新攻击面，需要针对性的防御机制。

Abstract: Model merging enables combining multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Prior work primarily attributes merging risks to unsafe constituent models, implicitly assuming that merging individually aligned models preserves safety. In contrast, we show that model merging reveals a previously overlooked jailbreak risk rooted in the pretrained foundation model, even when all constituent models are individually safety-aligned. Motivated by this observation, we study a new threat setting where an attacker constructs jailbreak prompts that generalize across merged models sharing the same pretrained backbone, without access to the exact merging coefficients or constituent checkpoints. To exploit this phenomenon, we propose \textbf{Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)}, which formulates jailbreak generation as a min--max optimization over the merging space to produce transferable adversarial suffixes across merged model families. Experiments across diverse backbones and merging settings show that BAJ achieves consistently high transfer success rates and remains effective under existing defenses.

</details>


### [346] [Algorithmic Principles For Multiclass Learning Are Hard To Come By: Limits of Regularization and Proper Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26516)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Vatsal Sharan,Alec Sun,Shang-Hua Teng,Chang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多分类学习理论中"学习能否归约为proper learning"以及"proper/improper学习能否被正则化器刻画"两个基本问题，并以三个否定性结果解决了此前工作中的三个开放问题：存在可学习但无法嵌入任何properly learnable类的问题；proper学习在训练误差上存在精细刻画（任何properly learnable类都有o(m)误差的proper学习者，但任意给定的亚线性尺度都可能是必要的）；存在无法被任何SRM学习者或局部正则化器学习的学习类。此外还给出了SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并通过"显示偏好的可积性"刻画了SRM可表示性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习理论的两个基本问题是"哪些预测问题可学习"（已有组合维数等优美解答）和"应当如何学习"（后者远未解决）。已知的通用多分类学习者都依赖对指数大小的一包含结构进行复杂定向，而proper learning与正则化等熟悉算法原理仍缺乏理解。作者由此追问：学习能否通过扩大假设类归约为proper learning，以及proper/improper多分类学习能否被合适的正则化器所刻画——这些问题源自此前工作的三个开放问题。

Method: 作者通过构造反例来给出否定性结果：(1) 构造一个可学习的多分类问题并证明它不能被嵌入任何properly learnable类（即通过扩大假设类无法将学习归约为proper learning）；(2) 对训练误差进行精确刻画——证明每个properly learnable类都允许一个在规模为m的样本上仅犯o(m)个错误的proper学习者，同时证明对任意给定的亚线性尺度a_m=o(m)，都存在某个properly learnable问题使该尺度是必要的；(3) 构造一个不能被任何SRM学习者学习的properly learnable类，以及一个不能被任何局部正则化器学习的可学习类。在正面结果方面，作者给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并利用显示偏好的可积性刻画SRM可表示性。

Result: 三个开放问题均以否定方式解决：其一，学习不能通过扩大假设类归约为proper learning，因为存在可学习但不可嵌入任何properly learnable类的问题；其二，proper学习可能要求训练误差，且该现象被精确刻画：upper bound为所有properly learnable类都存在o(m)误差的proper学习者，lower bound为任意预定的亚线性尺度a_m=o(m)都是某类properly learnable问题所必需的；其三，正则化并非通用学习者：存在无法被任何SRM学习者学习的properly learnable类，以及无法被任何局部正则化器学习的可学习类。正面地，作者给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并以显示偏好的可积性刻画SRM可表示性。

Conclusion: 本文表明"学习归约为proper learning"与"用正则化器刻画学习"这两条直观路径在一般多分类意义下均不成立，从而深化了对"如何学习"这一问题的理解：proper learning即便扩大假设类也并非通用归约目标，且训练误差与可学习性之间存在精细的必然张力。同时，SRM可学习性的充分条件与SRM可表示性的刻画表明，在下层结构受限的适当条件下，正则化原则仍可恢复其理论地位。这些结果共同为多分类学习理论中学习者的通用构造与算法的原理性理解提供了新的边界。

Abstract: Two of the most fundamental questions in statistical learning theory are the following: which prediction problems are learnable, and how should they be learned? For the former, elegant answers often take the form of combinatorial dimensions. The latter question, however, has proved considerably more elusive: all known general-purpose multiclass learners rely on intricate orientations of exponentially large one-inclusion structures, and familiar algorithmic principles such as proper learning and regularization remain poorly understood. Motivated by prior work, we ask whether learning reduces to proper learning---possibly over a larger hypothesis class---and whether proper or improper multiclass learning can ultimately be captured by suitable regularizers.
  Our primary results answer both questions negatively, resolving three open problems from prior work. First, we exhibit a learnable multiclass problem that cannot be embedded in any properly learnable class, meaning learning cannot be reduced to proper learning by enlarging the hypothesis class. Second, we demonstrate that proper learning can require training error and characterize this phenomenon precisely: every properly learnable class admits a proper learner making $o(m)$ errors on samples of size $m$, but every prescribed sublinear scale $a_m=o(m)$ is necessary for some properly learnable problem. Third, regularization is not a general learner: we exhibit a properly learnable class that cannot be learned by any Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) learner, and a learnable class that cannot be learned by any local regularizer. We complement these impossibility results with a positive theory that gives two sufficient conditions for SRM learnability and characterizes SRM representability through integrability of revealed preferences.

</details>


### [347] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对激活函数导数满足阶乘增长界的宽随机神经网络，建立了其输入混合导数的高概率上界，核心结果是针对Xavier初始化的tanh网络：在隐藏层宽度满足一定条件时，以高概率对所有非空多指标u和所有x∈[0,1]^{n_0}同时成立估计|D^u R| ≤ C_0 |u|! (C_1 L)^{|u|-1} ∏ β_j。该界表明一阶导数界与深度无关，而|u|阶无平方混合导数至多随深度按L^{|u|-1}多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于权重矩阵欧式算子范数的直接确定性分析给出的导数界通常随深度指数增长，这过于悲观且无法反映宽网络的真实正则性。作者希望证明：对于足够宽的高斯网络，导数界可以被显著改进，从而获得更紧的Lipschitz常数界与加权Sobolev范数界，为基于拟蒙特卡洛(QMC)的训练分析提供正则性依据。

Method: 先用确定性欧式算子范数方法得到随深度指数增长的基准界；随后针对足够宽的高斯网络，将最高阶导数中线性项单独分离出来，并用可测有限网(measurable finite nets)控制相应切向方向，从而代替对整层Jacobian/权重矩阵的范数估计，获得随深度多项式增长的改进界。在tanh激活+Xavier初始化+高斯权重的具体框架下给出定量常数C,C_0,C_1及宽度条件。

Result: 证明存在常数C,C_0,C_1>0，使得当宽度n ≥ C(L^3 n_0^2(1+log n_0)+L^2(1+log(L/η)))时，以至少1-η的概率，对每个非空u⊆[n_0]和每个x∈[0,1]^{n_0}同时有|D^u R_{Φ^(L)}(x)| ≤ C_0 |u|! (C_1 L)^{|u|-1} ∏_{j∈u} β_j(η,n_0)。特别地，一阶导数界与深度L无关，|u|阶无平方混合导数除坐标因子外至多按L^{|u|-1}多项式增长。由此推出网络实现的欧式Lipschitz常数与加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 该结果大幅改进了宽随机网络导数界的深度依赖（从指数降到多项式），展示了宽高斯的正则化效应；所得的加权Sobolev范数界将导数估计与拟蒙特卡洛积分联系起来，说明此类正则性可进入基于QMC的训练分析之中，为后续理论研究提供了工具与定量刻画。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [348] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"可量化性"（后续剂量-反应测试能否产生可用药效值）作为独立于"生物活性"的筛选分流目标，并证明可量化性可由前期低成本筛选特征（而非分子结构）强预测，从而优化昂贵的剂量-反应分析资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量药物筛选依赖低成本初级试验来优先选择化合物进行更昂贵的剂量-反应分析，最终以药效（potency）量化。现有筛选策略主要关注化合物在后续实验中能否确认生物活性，隐含假设确认活性就能得到可用的药效估计；但活性的确认并不必然意味着可量化的药效，因为活性化合物仍可能无法产生可报告的剂量-反应估计。因此需要将可量化性作为一个独立于生物活性的筛选目标来建模。

Method: 作者构建了一个"可量化性"建模框架，将其作为与生物活性不同的独立分诊目标；利用前期低成本筛选数据预测后续测试能否产生可用的药效估计，并考察不同预测信息来源（筛选特征 vs. 分子结构）的贡献；同时在未见过化学骨架和未见的测定机制家族上评估泛化性能。

Result: 可量化性可由前期低成本筛选强预测，且大部分预测信息来自观察到的筛选特征而非分子结构；基于响应的预测因子在未见过化学骨架和跨测定机制家族上保持稳健；成功量化的概率随响应幅度和测定背景显著变化。

Conclusion: 实验可测量性（区别于生物活性）是可以预测的筛选结果属性；可量化性感知的分诊策略能改善昂贵剂量-反应分析容量的分配效率。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [349] [Chart2SVG: Editable SVG Generation from Raster Chart Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26544)
*Jinning Cui,Lu Chen,Haoyan Shi,Yue He,Chenglong Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chart2SVG，一种多模态大语言模型，可将静态栅格图表转换为结构清晰、语义丰富且支持程序化编辑的 SVG。通过引入图表专属语义 token、构建 33K 的 Beagle+ 数据集、结合专门训练目标与渲染感知的后训练阶段，并构建图表结构图（CSG）以暴露视觉依赖，模型在重建保真度和下游编辑实用性上显著超越基线，推动智能交互式可视化工具的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态栅格图表（raster charts）缺乏结构化信息，难以进行程序化编辑、交互式探索和复用。现有方法难以同时恢复图表的几何图元与其功能角色，且缺乏可支撑稳健结构恢复的训练数据与高层级操作机制，因此需要一种能够生成既有视觉准确性又有结构一致性的可编辑表示的方法。

Method: 1) 提出 Chart2SVG，一种将图表专属语义 token 融入视觉-语言模型的多模态大语言模型，同时捕捉几何图元及其功能角色；2) 构建 Beagle+ 数据集（33K 个经过规范化与结构蒸馏的图表样本）以支持稳健的结构恢复；3) 结合专门训练目标与渲染感知（rendering-aware）的后训练阶段，生成视觉准确且结构一致的 SVG；4) 构建图表结构图（Chart Structure Graph, CSG）以暴露视觉依赖，支持交互式探索、图表再利用和布局复用等高层级操作。

Result: 实验表明 Chart2SVG 在重建保真度（reconstruction fidelity）与下游编辑实用性（downstream editing utility）方面显著优于基线方法，验证了其在结构与语义层面恢复图表的能力。

Conclusion: Chart2SVG 通过将语义感知的结构恢复、高质量数据集与可视化结构图相结合，提供了一种从静态图表到可编辑、可交互 SVG 的有效途径，推进了智能化和交互式可视化工具的开发。

Abstract: We present Chart2SVG, a multimodal large language model that converts static raster charts into structurally organized, semantically enriched SVGs that support programmatic editing. By incorporating chart-specific semantic tokens into a vision-language model, Chart2SVG captures both geometric primitives and their functional roles. To support robust structural recovery, we introduce Beagle+, a dataset of 33K canonicalized and structurally distilled chart samples. Our approach combines specialized training objectives with a rendering-aware post-training phase, producing SVGs that are both visually accurate and structurally consistent. To facilitate higher-level manipulations, we construct a Chart Structure Graph (CSG) that exposes visual dependencies, enabling tasks such as interactive exploration, chart repurposing, and layout reuse. Experiments show that Chart2SVG substantially outperforms baselines in reconstruction fidelity and downstream editing utility, advancing the development of intelligent and interactive visualization tools.

</details>


### [350] [Arrive and Survive: Scaling Safe Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning from One-Bit Failure Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26571)
*Guopeng Li,Yiyang Duan,Yiru Jiao,Chengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在带失败终止（failure-terminated）的马尔可夫决策过程中，现有对比强化学习（CRL）构造正样本时仅考虑失败前的未来目标，忽略了失败终止所移除的概率质量，导致目标达成价值估计存在系统性高估偏差。作者提出两种最小化修正——质量加权InfoNCE与对数存活质量分数，构成Safe-CRL方法，仅需失败终止提供的单比特信号即可实现安全的目标条件策略学习，并在12个失败易发的机器人导航与运动任务中显著提升生存率与目标达成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比强化学习（CRL）在目标条件任务中通过自监督对比目标实现高效策略学习，但在失败终止型MDP中，现有CRL只将失败前的未来目标用于构造正样本，未考虑失败终止移除的概率质量。理论分析表明这会造成目标达成价值的系统性高估偏差：接近失败的轨迹尽管保留的未来占用很少，却提供过强的成功监督信号，不安全动作可能通过灾难性失败引导被强化，导致策略学习失败和不可持续的目标达成行为。

Method: 作者提出Safe-CRL，包含两个最小而有效的修正：1）质量加权InfoNCE（mass-weighted InfoNCE），在评论家学习中对短存活未来的过重权重进行校正；2）对数存活质量分数（log-survival-mass score），在策略优化中恢复缺失的存活质量。该方法仅依赖失败终止提供的单比特信号即可扩展安全的目标条件策略学习。

Result: 在12个失败易发的机器人导航与运动任务中，Safe-CRL在生存率上持续改进，并在目标达成性能上显著优于Scaling-CRL基线；深层Safe-CRL策略还展现出复杂的避障/失败规避行为。

Conclusion: 该研究补全了失败终止条件下CRL的理论，并提供了一个可扩展的安全强化学习框架，验证了仅凭失败终止信号即可实现安全且高效的目标条件策略学习。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) scales effectively in goal-conditioned tasks by casting policy learning into a self-supervised contrastive objective. However, in a failure-terminated Markov decision process, established CRL considers pre-failure future goals only when constructing positive samples, without accounting for the probability mass removed by failure termination. Our theoretical analysis shows that this omission induces a systematic overestimation bias in goal-reaching values. Consequently, near-failure trajectories provide disproportionately strong supervision of success despite retaining little future occupancy. Unsafe actions can thereby be reinforced through catastrophic failure bootstrapping, leading to failed policy learning and unsustainable goal-reaching behaviours. To address this problem, we introduce two minimal yet strong corrections: mass-weighted InfoNCE corrects the overweighting of short surviving futures in critic learning, and a log-survival-mass score restores the missing survival mass in policy optimization. The resulting method, Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL), requires only the one-bit signal provided by failure termination to scale safe goal-conditioned policy learning. Across twelve failure-prone robot navigation and locomotion tasks, Safe-CRL consistently improves survival and substantially outperforms the Scaling-CRL baseline in goal-reaching performance. Additionally, deep Safe-CRL policies exhibit complex failure-avoidance behaviours. This study completes the CRL theory under failure termination and provides a scalable safe RL framework. The code is available via https://github.com/RomainLITUD/safe-crl.

</details>


### [351] [Activation Outliers Matter: Robust Recovery for Quantized Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581)
*Tanzila Rahman,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Zhuang Ma,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Yao Wang,Xing Huang,Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Yonghan Dong,Wei Guo,Yaoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中的低比特量化方案，发现MXFP8近乎无损而4比特量化性能显著下降，并通过消融实验识别出激活量化是性能损失的主要来源。据此提出残差回退量化（RFQ），通过辅助量化残差通路补偿激活量化误差，在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上验证了其能显著恢复MXFP4和HiF4量化下损失的性能，缩小与BF16基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化可降低MLLM的计算和内存开销，且硬件已支持MXFP8到MXFP4、HiF4等低精度格式，但缺乏对这些量化方案在MLLM上的系统性研究，且激进4比特量化带来的性能退化原因尚未明确。

Method: 首先对覆盖视频生成和推理任务的代表性MLLM进行系统量化分析；通过大量消融实验定位性能损失来源（发现激活量化为主因，远超权重量化）；基于此提出残差回退量化（RFQ），即在超低比特激活表示之外增加一个辅助量化残差通路，显式建模并补偿量化误差，无需修改架构且计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上的实验表明，RFQ持续恢复了MXFP4和HiF4量化下的大部分性能损失，显著缩小了与BF16基线在生成任务和4个推理基准上的差距。

Conclusion: 研究确立了激活量化是超低比特MLLM性能退化的主导瓶颈，并证明基于残差的激活重建是稳健4比特部署的有效且实用策略。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [352] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出J-Zero，一个统一的Challenger–Solver–Judge三方协同进化框架，使语言模型在可验证与不可验证领域均能实现无需人工标注的自我进化；其中Judge通过利用具有先验已知顺序的偏好对（Solver答案优于Challenger答案、分解重组答案优于一次性答案）进行协同适应，从而避免打分漂移与退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型有望以降低人工监督成本的方式通向超级智能，但现有工作主要集中在可验证领域，对不可验证领域的自我进化探索不足，且基线方法在多轮迭代后性能退化。

Method: 提出J-Zero统一框架：Challenger与Solver通过对抗式交互协同进化——Challenger生成难度递增的任务，Solver学习产出更高质量的答案；同时Judge基于先验顺序已知的偏好对（而非其自身打分）进行协同适应，偏好来源为Solver回答优于Challenger回答，以及分解重组后的回答优于一次性回答。

Result: J-Zero在可验证领域平均超过基线4.2分，在不可验证领域平均超过8.0分，且能在至少十轮迭代中持续提升，而基线在两轮后即出现退化。

Conclusion: J-Zero通过三方协同进化与基于数据内在顺序的Judge适应机制，实现了对可验证和不可验证领域统一、稳定的自我改进，有效抑制了进化过程中的退化问题。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [353] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimCast-S2S，一种基于潜空间扩散生成模型（latent diffusion）的概率性次季节-季节（S2S）降水预报框架，结合变分自编码器（VAE）潜空间与基于 LoRA 的模拟数据到再分析数据的迁移学习，在仅使用部分大气变量且无需后处理的情况下，其性能优于深度学习方法基线，并与 ECMWF-S2S 等业务系统相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S 降水预报具有重要的金融和社会影响，但存在预测信号弱、不确定性高、业务系统计算成本大等挑战。同时，S2S 预报需要不确定性量化而非仅确定性点预报，而扩散模型需要大量训练数据，且在大物理空间生成大规模概率集合的计算成本高昂，这些瓶颈促使作者设计新的数据驱动方案。

Method: 提出 SimCast-S2S 生成式潜扩散框架：1) 采用基于扩散的生成式流程，从条件分布中有效采样以进行概率预报；2) 在由变分自编码器（VAE）学习的紧凑潜空间中运行，实现高效的大规模集合生成；3) 通过低秩自适应（LoRA）迁移学习解决数据不足问题——先在气候模拟大集合上预训练，再在有限的再分析数据上微调。

Result: 在再分析数据上，SimCast-S2S 优于 CNN 和 U-Net 等深度学习基线；尽管仅使用部分大气输入变量且无后处理、偏差订正或校准，它仍与 ECMWF-S2S 等先进业务系统相当，并在多数情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 潜空间生成建模与模拟到再分析的迁移学习相结合，为数据驱动的概率性 S2S 降水预报提供了一条高效且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [354] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在互补的可再生能源场景下（大规模WEC数据集、16机WEC数据集、Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA数据）系统基准测试了传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习方法，发现不同数据形态下最优模型各异：树集成（Extra Trees）最适合结构化WEC布局数据（相对MLP的MAE降低约63.7%），STGCN适合显式空间-时间交互学习（MAE≈167.0 kW，R=0.93），RF-BiLSTM混合模型综合精度最佳（MAE=150.5 kW，较LSTM降低约75%，较STGCN提升约10%），并指出不存在普遍最优的单一AI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源预测与代理建模场景多样，涵盖结构化布局数据、高维WEC场数据以及含时空交互的风电场时序数据，但缺乏对这些AI架构（从传统ML到Transformer、图模型、混合集成）在互补场景下的系统性基准比较，难以确定何种架构最适合何种数据形态。

Method: 对传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、基于图的模型以及混合集成深度学习在三个数据集（大规模WEC数据集、16机WEC数据集、Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA数据）上进行全面基准测试与对比。

Result: 结构化WEC布局数据上，随机分区与提升方法可有效捕捉非线性布局功率交互，Extra Trees为最强模型，较MLP基线MAE降低约63.7%；STGCN通过显式学习空间与时间涡轮交互，MAE降至约167.0 kW、R=0.93；RF-BiLSTM混合模型取得最佳整体预测精度，MAE=150.5 kW，较独立LSTM降低约75%，并较STGCN提升约10%。实验表明没有单一AI架构普遍最优。

Conclusion: 不同数据特征对应不同最优架构：随机化和提升树集成适合结构化WEC代理建模，图网络在显式空间交互占主导时更具优势，而集成循环混合模型（如RF-BiLSTM）在非线性表格关系与时间动态共存时达到最优平衡；因此应依据数据形态选择合适的AI架构而非依赖单一通用模型。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [355] [Robust Neural Stimulation Response Modeling Through Meta-Learning and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26649)
*Matthew J Bryan,Daniel C Muir,Felix Schwock,Azadeh Yazdan-Shahmorad,Rajesh P N Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明元学习（MAML）和跨会话预训练可应用于神经刺激响应建模：将时间基函数模型（TBFM）扩展为基于MAML的跨会话预训练架构，在两只非人灵长类初级感觉运动皮层光遗传刺激的40个会话上，将灾难性预测失败（测试R²<0.05）的会话数从16/40降至1/40，预测区间显著收窄，并在同等精度下将标定需求减少50-90%，证明刺激响应的跨会话结构足以支撑预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动的闭环神经刺激（如帕金森病治疗、感觉修复）受两大障碍制约：1) 预测刺激后果的预测模型在相当比例的会话中灾难性失效；2) 逐会话标定要求常与临床约束不兼容。本文旨在通过首次将元学习与预训练应用于神经刺激响应建模来同时解决上述两个问题。

Method: 基于时间基函数模型（TBFM）预测状态依赖的神经刺激响应，并用一种基于模型无关元学习（MAML）的新型架构与算法对其进行跨会话预训练扩展。在两只因非人灵长类初级感觉运动皮层的40个光遗传刺激会话上进行评估，对比单会话训练与MAML预训练的性能。

Result: 元学习显著降低灾难性预测失败：标定集大小为1k时，测试R²<0.05的会话从16/40（单会话训练）降至1（MAML预训练），且预测区间显著变窄（p<0.05）。在精度匹配条件下标定需求减少50-90%，使在临床会话时间约束下原本不可行的实验成为可能。

Conclusion: 结果表明刺激响应的跨会话结构足够一致以支持预训练，提供了元学习方法对神经刺激可行的首个经验证据。其鲁棒性与样本效率提升直接应对了部署模型驱动刺激控制器的已知障碍，并激励社区构建标准化多站点刺激数据集、进一步探索用于稳健闭环刺激的元学习方法。

Abstract: Objective: Model-based closed-loop neural stimulation holds promise for therapeutic applications ranging from Parkinson's disease to sensory restoration, but deployment has been limited by two obstacles: 1) forecasting models for predicting the consequences of stimulation fail catastrophically on a meaningful fraction of sessions, and 2) per-session calibration requirements are often incompatible with clinical constraints. We address both by demonstrating, for the first time, that meta-learning and pretraining can be applied to neural stimulation response modeling. Methods: Temporal basis function models (TBFMs) forecast state-dependent neural responses to stimulation. We extend TBFMs with cross-session pretraining using a novel architecture and algorithm based on model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML), evaluating them on 40 sessions of optogenetic stimulation in primary sensorimotor cortex of two non-human primates. Results: Meta-learning substantially reduces catastrophic forecast failure: for a 1k calibration set size, sessions with test R-squared < 0.05 drop from 16 of 40 (single-session training) to 1 (MAML-pretrained), and prediction intervals become significantly narrower (p < 0.05). Calibration requirements are reduced by 50-90% at matched accuracy, enabling experiments otherwise infeasible within clinical session-time constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate that cross-session structure in stimulation responses is consistent enough to support pretraining, providing the first empirical evidence that meta-learning approaches are viable for neural stimulation. Significance: The robustness and sample efficiency gains directly address known obstacles to deploying model-based stimulation controllers. Our results motivate community efforts to assemble standardized multi-site stimulation datasets and to further explore meta-learning for robust closed-loop stimulation.

</details>


### [356] [When Privacy Hurts Mergeability: Geometry-Aware Model Merging under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26655)
*Jin Liu,Junkang Liu,Ning Xi,Yinbin Miao,Dawei Wei,Ke Cheng,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究差分隐私（DP）微调模型合并中的几何特性，识别出本地尖锐性（local sharpness）与参考漂移（reference drift）两大几何障碍，并提出几何感知框架DP-Merging，通过DP兼容的锐度感知目标与基于参考的对齐正则化，在保持隐私保障的前提下显著提升私有任务模型合并后的多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可以在不访问原始任务数据的情况下，将多个独立微调的任务模型构建为单一多任务模型，适用于任务数据无法集中的场景。然而发布的任务模型仍可能泄露私有微调数据，差分隐私为限制此类泄露提供了原则性机制，但其对模型合并的影响尚未被充分理解。因此需要研究差分隐私模型合并的几何特性，找出私有任务模型难以合并的原因。

Method: 提出DP-Merging几何感知框架：其一，采用DP兼容的锐度感知目标（sharpness-aware objective）引导每个私有任务模型收敛到更平坦的损失区域，以缓解局部尖锐性；其二，引入基于参考的对齐正则化器（reference-based alignment regularizer），使任务模型保持接近共享预训练初始化，以抑制参考漂移。此外，推导了合并间隙上界，证明降低局部曲率与参考漂移可收紧合并所导致损失增加的上界。

Result: 在多个隐私预算下，针对视觉与语言任务的实验表明，DP-Merging一致地提升了私有合并模型的性能，同时保留了底层DP微调过程的隐私保障；实验结果验证了减少局部曲率与参考漂移的作用。

Conclusion: 研究工作揭示了差分隐私模型合并中的两大几何障碍（局部尖锐性与参考漂移），并证明通过几何感知的训练引导（平坦区域导向与参考对齐）能够在不牺牲隐私保证的前提下有效改善DP任务模型的可合并性，为隐私保护下的多任务模型融合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Model merging promises to construct a single multi-task model from independently fine-tuned task models without accessing the original task data. This makes it attractive when task data cannot be centralized, but released task models may still leak private fine-tuning data. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled mechanism for limiting such leakage, yet its effect on model merging remains poorly understood. In this paper, we study the geometry of differentially private model merging and identify two geometric obstacles that make private task models difficult to merge: \emph{local sharpness}, which makes task losses sensitive to the parameter displacement induced by merging, and \emph{reference drift}, which measures the displacement of private task models from the shared pretrained initialization and amplifies cross-task interference. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{DP-Merging}, a geometry-aware framework that improves the mergeability of differentially private task models. DP-Merging uses a DP-compatible sharpness-aware objective to guide each private task model toward flatter loss regions, and a reference-based alignment regularizer to keep task models close to the shared pretrained initialization. We derive a merge-gap upper bound showing that reducing local curvature and reference drift tightens the bound on the loss increase induced by merging. Experiments on vision and language tasks across multiple privacy budgets show that DP-Merging consistently improves private merged-model performance while preserving the privacy guarantees of the underlying DP fine-tuning procedures.

</details>


### [357] [Simple Actors and Deep Critics for Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26659)
*Guhyeon Kang,Jaehwi Lee,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC（轻量Actor、深度Critic）方法，重新审视离线强化学习中actor-critic架构的容量分配问题。由于critic仅在训练时使用、actor在部署时每步运行，作者主张将更多容量分配给critic而非actor以提高推理效率。针对加深MLP critic在离线RL中出现的三种失败模式（优化不稳定、自举噪声放大、值域漂移），分别采用残差MLP骨干、n步自举目标、分类交叉熵损失加以解决，配合轻量确定性actor，在OGBench上匹敌最强的扩散/流匹配基线，同时推理延迟最高降低4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配等生成式actor虽能捕捉离线数据中的多模态行为，但每次动作都需要多次去噪或积分步骤，在部署时每一步决策都产生巨大开销。作者重新审视容量应投入何处：critic只在训练时使用、部署时被丢弃，而actor在每一步都要运行，因此将容量分配给critic而非actor更有利于推理效率。然而，离线RL中加深MLP critic已知会引入多种不稳定，实践中迫使critic保持浅层，这构成了研究动机。

Method: 作者识别出离线RL中加深critic时的三种不同失败模式——优化（optimization）失败、自举噪声放大（bootstrap-noise amplification）和值域漂移（value-range drift），并针对每种失败使用相应组件应对：残差MLP骨干（residual MLP backbone）、n步自举目标（n-step bootstrap targets）和分类交叉熵损失（categorical cross-entropy loss）。将这些组件与轻量确定性actor结合，提出LAC（Light Actor, deep Critic）方法。

Result: 在OGBench基准上，LAC匹配了最强的扩散和流匹配基线，同时实现最高4倍的推理延迟降低，其性能可与无需蒸馏的一步蒸馏策略相当。此外，该critic配方可跨actor参数化迁移（transfer across actor parametrizations）。

Conclusion: 通过将容量从actor重新分配并投向深层critic，并针对性地解决深层critic的三种失败模式，LAC在保持与强生成式基线相当性能的同时大幅降低了部署时的推理延迟。该critic配方具有通用性，可迁移到不同actor参数化，为离线RL中兼顾能力与效率的架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent progress in offline reinforcement learning (RL) has been driven by expressive generative actors such as diffusion and flow-matching policies, which capture multimodal behavior in offline datasets. However, these actors require multiple denoising or integration steps per action and thus incur substantial overhead at every decision in deployment. In this work, we revisit where capacity should be invested in an offline actor--critic method. Since the critic is used only during training and is discarded at deployment while the actor runs at every decision step, allocating capacity to the critic rather than the actor is more favorable for inference-time efficiency. However, scaling MLP critics in offline RL is known to introduce several distinct instabilities that have, in practice, kept critics shallow. We identify three distinct failure modes that arise when critics are deepened in offline RL---optimization, bootstrap-noise amplification, and value-range drift---and address each with a corresponding ingredient: a residual MLP backbone, n-step bootstrap targets, and a categorical cross-entropy loss. Combining these ingredients with a lightweight deterministic actor, we propose LAC (Light Actor, deep Critic). On OGBench, LAC matches the strongest diffusion- and flow-matching baselines while achieving up to 4x lower inference latency, comparable to one-step distilled policies without distillation. Its critic recipe also transfers across actor parametrizations.

</details>


### [358] [Neural Regression with Embeddings for Numerical Attribute Prediction in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26729)
*Rupesh Sapkota,Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou,Moshood Yekini,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LitEm，一个用于预测知识图谱中数值属性的神经回归模型，使直推式知识图谱嵌入模型能够处理数值属性；并提出一个联合训练（co-training）框架，在提升链接预测性能（尤其对双线性模型）的同时赋予模型数值属性预测能力。在 FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K 和 Mutagenesis 上取得最佳或次佳结果，且证明性能提升源于对属性的"字面感知"（literal-aware）编码，而非仅靠增加参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直推式知识图谱嵌入模型已广泛应用于链接预测和查询回答，但知识图谱中常包含丰富的数值属性，大多数嵌入模型忽略这些属性，限制了其表示包含多样化信息的真实世界知识图谱的能力。

Method: 提出神经回归模型 LitEm，用于预测知识图谱中的数值属性；同时提出一个联合训练（co-training）框架，将 LitEm 与最先进的直推式知识图谱嵌入模型联合训练，使嵌入模型在保留原有功能的同时获得数值属性预测能力。

Result: 在 FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K 和 Mutagenesis 数据集上，LitEm 在大部分数值属性上取得最佳或次佳结果；联合训练主要提升了双线性模型的链接预测性能，并使这些模型能够预测数值属性。字面感知评估表明，联合训练帮助模型以"literal-aware"方式编码和利用属性信息，性能提升并非仅仅来自额外参数。

Conclusion: 联合训练能够真正帮助嵌入模型编码并利用数值属性信息，带来实质性的链接预测改进；作者公开了实现代码（https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings）以便复现和进一步研究。
